=== C++: full body of CfdSimulation<Real>::writeFieldSnapshots(), and its call site in run() ===
     1	void CfdSimulation<Real>::writeFieldSnapshots() {
     2	    int nx = cfg_.Nx, ny = cfg_.Ny, nz = cfg_.Nz;
     3	
     4	    std::vector<Real> tmprHost = temperature_.downloadCurrent(*hand_);
     5	    std::vector<Real> vxHost = vx_.downloadCurrent(*hand_);
     6	    std::vector<Real> vyHost = vy_.downloadCurrent(*hand_);
     7	    std::vector<Real> vzHost = vz_.downloadCurrent(*hand_);
     8	    std::vector<Real> presHost = pressure_.downloadTo(*hand_);
     9	    std::vector<Real> potentialHost = potential_.buffer().downloadTo(*hand_);
    10	
    11	    FieldView<Real> tmprView(tmprHost.data(), nx + 1, ny + 1, nz + 1);
    12	    FieldView<Real> vxView(vxHost.data(), nx, ny + 1, nz + 1);
    13	    FieldView<Real> vyView(vyHost.data(), nx + 1, ny, nz + 1);
    14	    FieldView<Real> vzView(vzHost.data(), nx + 1, ny + 1, nz);
    15	    // Potential's own real extent is (Nx,Ny+1,Nz) -- GpuSolverSetup.cu's
    16	    // own comment at its construction -- not the same as any of the four
    17	    // views above.
    18	    FieldView<Real> potentialView(potentialHost.data(), nx, ny + 1, nz);
    19	
    20	    AxisBoundaries temperatureBc = temperatureBoundaries<Real>(cfg_);
    21	    AxisBoundaries potentialBc = potentialBoundaries<Real>(cfg_);
    22	    std::vector<Real> buoyancyTable = computeBuoyancyTable<Real>(cfg_, mesh_);
    23	
    24	    // Tmpr.dat: Tmpr + Teta, matching the original's own `Tmpr = Tmpr +
    25	    // Teta` immediately before its Point_Write call (Solution_time_Q2D.f90
    26	    // line 128) -- same "total temperature" convention Tmpr_av.dat already
    27	    // uses, just from the instantaneous field instead of the time-average.
    28	    std::vector<Real> tmprFull = resolveGhostInclusive<Real>(nx + 2, ny + 2, nz + 2,
    29	        [&](int i, int j, int k) { return BoundaryConditions::readTemperature(tmprView, i, j, k, temperatureBc) + buoyancyTable[i]; });
    30	    std::vector<Real> vxFull = resolveGhostInclusive<Real>(nx + 1, ny + 2, nz + 2,
    31	        [&](int i, int j, int k) { return BoundaryConditions::readVelocity(vxView, i, j, k); });
    32	    std::vector<Real> vyFull = resolveGhostInclusive<Real>(nx + 2, ny + 1, nz + 2,
    33	        [&](int i, int j, int k) { return BoundaryConditions::readVelocity(vyView, i, j, k); });
    34	    std::vector<Real> vzFull = resolveGhostInclusive<Real>(nx + 2, ny + 2, nz + 1,
    35	        [&](int i, int j, int k) { return BoundaryConditions::readVelocity(vzView, i, j, k); });
    36	    // readTemperature(), not readVelocity(): field-agnostic despite the
    37	    // name (its own body is just FieldView+AxisBoundaries, nothing
    38	    // temperature-specific) -- the right one here since Potential, like
    39	    // Temperature, has a real configured Dirichlet/Neumann boundary kind,
    40	    // unlike velocity's unconditional no-slip zero. No buoyancy term:
    41	    // Teta is a temperature-only reference profile.
    42	    std::vector<Real> potentialFull = resolveGhostInclusive<Real>(nx + 1, ny + 2, nz + 1,
    43	        [&](int i, int j, int k) { return BoundaryConditions::readTemperature(potentialView, i, j, k, potentialBc); });
    44	
    45	    const std::vector<Real>& x12 = mesh_.hostNodeHalfX();
    46	    const std::vector<Real>& y12 = mesh_.hostNodeHalfY();
    47	    const std::vector<Real>& z12 = mesh_.hostNodeHalfZ();
    48	    const std::vector<Real>& xPlain = mesh_.hostX();
    49	    const std::vector<Real>& yPlain = mesh_.hostY();
    50	    const std::vector<Real>& zPlain = mesh_.hostZ();
    51	
    52	    writeTecplot3D(outputPath("Tmpr.dat"), nx + 2, ny + 2, nz + 2, x12, y12, z12, tmprFull, "Tmpr      ");
    53	    writeTecplot3D(outputPath("Vx.dat"),   nx + 1, ny + 2, nz + 2, xPlain, y12, z12, vxFull, "Vx        ");
    54	    writeTecplot3D(outputPath("Vy.dat"),   nx + 2, ny + 1, nz + 2, x12, yPlain, z12, vyFull, "Vy        ");
    55	    writeTecplot3D(outputPath("Vz.dat"),   nx + 2, ny + 2, nz + 1, x12, y12, zPlain, vzFull, "Vz        ");
    56	    writeTecplot3D(outputPath("Potential.dat"), nx + 1, ny + 2, nz + 1, xPlain, y12, zPlain, potentialFull, "Potential ");
    57	
    58	    // Pres.dat: pressure_'s own real extent, no ghost resolution at all --
    59	    // see this method's doc comment in CfdSimulation.h for why that's
    60	    // correct here, not an omission (confirmed against every real Fortran
    61	    // Prs usage, not just this one disabled call). presHost's layout (i
    62	    // fastest, from FieldView's own index formula) already matches
    63	    // writeTecplot3D's values[idx++] order exactly, so it's passed through
    64	    // unmodified -- no resolveGhostInclusive step for this one field. x12/
    65	    // y12/z12 sliced to the same real-extent range (dropping the ghost
    66	    // indices every other field's write needs), matching the original's
    67	    // own X12(1:Nx1)/Y12(1:Ny1)/Z12(1:Nz1) argument slice.
    68	    std::vector<Real> presX(x12.begin() + 1, x12.begin() + 1 + (nx + 1));
    69	    std::vector<Real> presY(y12.begin() + 1, y12.begin() + 1 + (ny + 1));
    70	    std::vector<Real> presZ(z12.begin() + 1, z12.begin() + 1 + (nz + 1));
    71	    writeTecplot3D(outputPath("Pres.dat"), nx, ny, nz, presX, presY, presZ, presHost, "Prs       ");
    72	}
    73	
    74	template<typename Real>
    75	void CfdSimulation<Real>::run() {
    76	    openOutputFiles();
    77	
    78	    Real targetTime = elapsedTime_ + (Real)cfg_.runDuration;
    79	    Real timeStep = (Real)cfg_.timeStep;
    80	    std::string checkpointPath = outputPath("checkpoint.cfdc");
    81	
    82	    while (true) {
    83	        ++iteration_;
    84	        elapsedTime_ += timeStep;
    85	
    86	        Real rnsX, rnsY, rnsZ, rTmpr, rdp;
    87	        runOneTimeStep(rnsX, rnsY, rnsZ, rTmpr, rdp);
    88	
    89	        // Serie.dat/History.dat: unconditional in the original (no Iprint
    90	        // gate -- see cfg_.writeSerieAndHistory's doc comment), but unlike
    91	        // the original's plain CPU arithmetic on already-resident memory,
    92	        // computeGlobalDiagnostics() here costs a real per-iteration GPU
    93	        // tax (handle synchs plus full-volume reductions plus blocking
    94	        // downloads). Gated on cfg_.writeSerieAndHistory so that tax is
    95	        // skipped entirely -- not just the two files left unwritten --
    96	        // when the data isn't wanted. serieFile_/historyFile_ are only
    97	        // ever non-null when this flag is set (see openOutputFiles()).
    98	        if (cfg_.writeSerieAndHistory) {
    99	            Real kineticEnergy, nusselt, probeVz, probeTmpr;
   100	            computeGlobalDiagnostics(kineticEnergy, nusselt, probeVz, probeTmpr);
   101	            std::fprintf(serieFile_.get(), "%.15g %.15g %.15g\n", (double)elapsedTime_, (double)kineticEnergy, (double)nusselt);
   102	            std::fflush(serieFile_.get());
   103	            std::fprintf(historyFile_.get(), "%.15g %.15g %.15g\n", (double)elapsedTime_, (double)probeVz, (double)probeTmpr);
   104	            std::fflush(historyFile_.get());
   105	        }
   106	
   107	        // Fourier_sum: independent of cfg_.writeSerieAndHistory above --
   108	        // in the original, I_Fourier and the Serie.dat/History.dat writes
   109	        // are unrelated flags, so this is its own top-level guard, not
   110	        // nested in the block above.
   111	        if (cfg_.timeAveragingEnabled) accumulateTimeAverage();
   112	
   113	        bool shouldPrint = (iteration_ % cfg_.printEvery) == 0;
   114	        if (shouldPrint) {
   115	            printIterationReport(elapsedTime_, targetTime, rnsX, rnsY, rnsZ, rTmpr, rdp);
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        // Independent of printEvery -- cfg_.checkpointEvery's own doc
   119	        // comment (SimulationConfig.h) already says so, but nothing
   120	        // actually read the field: checkpointing silently followed
   121	        // printEvery instead, same as the report above. With
   122	        // example_config.json's print_every=1, that meant downloading
   123	        // and writing a full checkpoint (every field, current AND old)
   124	        // every single iteration, not just every checkpointEvery.
   125	        bool shouldCheckpoint = (iteration_ % cfg_.checkpointEvery) == 0;
   126	        if (shouldCheckpoint) {
   127	            CheckpointIO::write(checkpointPath, cfg_.Nx, cfg_.Ny, cfg_.Nz,
   128	                temperature_, vx_, vy_, vz_, pressure_, elapsedTime_, *hand_);
   129	        }
   130	
   131	        Real worstResidual = std::max(std::max(rnsX, rnsY), std::max(rnsZ, rTmpr));
   132	        if (worstResidual < (Real)cfg_.epsConvergence) break;
   133	        if (!(elapsedTime_ < targetTime) || iteration_ >= cfg_.maxIterations) break;
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    CheckpointIO::write(checkpointPath, cfg_.Nx, cfg_.Ny, cfg_.Nz,
   137	        temperature_, vx_, vy_, vz_, pressure_, elapsedTime_, *hand_);
   138	
   139	    // Tmpr.dat/Vx.dat/Vy.dat/Vz.dat/Pres.dat/Potential.dat: the original's
   140	    // own disabled block sits right here too, between the final checkpoint
   141	    // and Plot_Amplitude (Solution_time_Q2D.f90 lines 128-133, right after
   142	    // label 880's Call Outp and before If(I_Fourier==0) Call Plot_
   143	    // Amplitude) -- same reasoning as the ordering note below: always the
   144	    // instantaneous state, the original's own Tmpr=Tmp_Av substitution
   145	    // (inside Plot_Amplitude) hasn't run yet at this point even when
   146	    // time-averaging is also enabled.
   147	    if (cfg_.writeFieldSnapshots) writeFieldSnapshots();
   148	
   149	    // Plot_Amplitude: called here, between the final checkpoint and the
   150	    // report, matching the original's exact ordering (Outp, then Plot_
   151	    // Amplitude, then the Nusselt/kinetic-energy report) -- the checkpoint
   152	    // is always the true instantaneous final state, never the time-
   153	    // average, in both the original and here.
   154	    if (cfg_.timeAveragingEnabled) finalizeTimeAverage();
   155	
   156	    printFinalReport();
   157	
   158	    // Average_flow: unconditional in the original, called once right

=== Fortran: full contents of Get_Potential_Finish (EM_forcing.f90, lines 59-195) ===
Subroutine Get_Potential_Finish(Istp)
    Use Grid
    Use Numbers
    Use Numerica
    Use Operators
    Use Variables
    Use AlexCudaCompatibility, only : PotentialHandle, Potential_pinned
    Use eigenbcgsolver_eigen_mod, only : synch_d

    Implicit Real(kind=8) (A-H,O-Z)

    ! Loop counters and the full declared bounds of Potential (0:Nxx1,
    ! 0:Nyy2,0:Nzz1 -- see the Allocate in ConvMain_3D_Q2D.f90). Pulled via
    ! LBound/UBound rather than hardcoded so every statement below touches
    ! exactly the same elements the original bare-colon ("Potential(:,:,:)")
    ! syntax did -- this is a parallelization/temp-array fix only, not a
    ! change to which cells get written.
    Integer :: i, j, k, iLo, iHi, jLo, jHi, kLo, kHi
    Real(kind=8) :: Pot000
! ___________________________________________________

        ! synch_d no longer takes an output argument: it just waits for the
        ! GPU work to finish. The solution is then read directly from
        ! Potential_pinned below -- same reasoning as Get_Potential_Launch,
        ! no separate GPU_SOL_Pot needed anymore.
        Call synch_d(PotentialHandle)

        ! Padded<->unpadded remap -- still needed, same as before (Potential's
        ! true declared shape (0:Nxx1,0:Nyy2,0:Nzz1) doesn't match this
        ! solve's region (1:Nx,1:Ny1,1:Nz), so this can't be a bare
        ! whole-array assignment without either being wrong or forcing a
        ! hidden compiler temporary). Just reads from Potential_pinned now
        ! instead of GPU_SOL_Pot.
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(i,j,k)
    Do i = 1, Nx
      Do j = 1, Ny1
        Do k = 1, Nz
          Potential(i,j,k) = Potential_pinned(i,j,k)
        End Do
      End Do
    End Do

        ! Was: Potential = Potential - Potential(1,1,1)
        ! Bare colons here default to Potential's FULL declared bounds
        ! (0:Nxx1,0:Nyy2,0:Nzz1), not just the active (Nx,Ny1,Nz) region --
        ! so this one statement was walking the entire preallocated buffer.
        ! Fortran's array-assignment semantics guarantee the whole RHS
        ! (including Potential(1,1,1)) is evaluated using the pre-assignment
        ! values before anything is written, so the scalar is captured once,
        ! up front, to reproduce that exactly under explicit looping/OpenMP.
        Pot000 = Potential(1,1,1)
        iLo = LBound(Potential,1);  iHi = UBound(Potential,1)
        jLo = LBound(Potential,2);  jHi = UBound(Potential,2)
        kLo = LBound(Potential,3);  kHi = UBound(Potential,3)

!$OMP Parallel Do Private(i,j,k)
    Do i = iLo, iHi
      Do j = jLo, jHi
        Do k = kLo, kHi
          Potential(i,j,k) = Potential(i,j,k) - Pot000
        End Do
      End Do
    End Do

        If (Istp == 10) Then
            Write(*,*) ' DIAG PotSolved(2,Ny1/2,2)=', Potential(2,Ny1/2,2)
            Write(*,*) ' DIAG PotSolved(Nx-1,Ny1/2,2)=', Potential(Nx-1,Ny1/2,2)
            Write(*,*) ' DIAG PotSolved(2,Ny1/2,Nz-1)=', Potential(2,Ny1/2,Nz-1)
            Write(*,*) ' DIAG PotSolved(Nx/2,Ny1/2,Nz/2)=', Potential(Nx/2,Ny1/2,Nz/2)
        End If

    If(EVD_Pot_X == 1 ) then
        ! Was: Potential(0,:,:) = Potential(1,:,:);  Potential(Nx1,:,:) = Potential(Nx,:,:)
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(j,k)
        Do j = jLo, jHi
          Do k = kLo, kHi
             Potential(0,j,k)   = Potential(1,j,k)
             Potential(Nx1,j,k) = Potential(Nx,j,k)
          End Do
        End Do
     else
        ! Was: Potential(0,:,:) = 0.d0;  Potential(Nx1,:,:) = 0.d0
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(j,k)
        Do j = jLo, jHi
          Do k = kLo, kHi
             Potential(0,j,k)   = 0.d0
             Potential(Nx1,j,k) = 0.d0
          End Do
        End Do
    End If

     If(EVD_Pot_Y == 1 ) then
        ! Was: Potential(:,0,:) = Potential(:,1,:);  Potential(:,Ny2,:) = Potential(:,Ny1,:)
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(i,k)
       Do i = iLo, iHi
         Do k = kLo, kHi
            Potential(i,0,k)   = Potential(i,1,k)
            Potential(i,Ny2,k) = Potential(i,Ny1,k)
         End Do
       End Do
     else
        ! Was: Potential(:,0,:) = 0.d0;  Potential(:,Ny2,:) = 0.d0
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(i,k)
        Do i = iLo, iHi
          Do k = kLo, kHi
             Potential(i,0,k)   = 0.d0
             Potential(i,Ny2,k) = 0.d0
          End Do
        End Do
    End If

     If(EVD_Pot_Z == 1 ) then
        ! Was: Potential(:,:,0) = Potential(:,:,1);  Potential(:,:,Nz1) = Potential(:,:,Nz)
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(i,j)
        Do i = iLo, iHi
          Do j = jLo, jHi
             Potential(i,j,0)   = Potential(i,j,1)
             Potential(i,j,Nz1) = Potential(i,j,Nz)
          End Do
        End Do
     else
        ! Was: Potential(:,:,0) = 0.d0;  Potential(:,:,Nz1) = 0.d0
!$OMP Parallel Do Private(i,j)
        Do i = iLo, iHi
          Do j = jLo, jHi
             Potential(i,j,0)   = 0.d0
             Potential(i,j,Nz1) = 0.d0
          End Do
        End Do
    End If

  Return
End Subroutine Get_Potential_Finish

=== Fortran: full contents of Plot_Amplitude and Fourier_sum (Solution_time_Q2D.f90, lines 135-223) ===

    Subroutine Fourier_sum

        Tmp_Amplitude(0,:,:,:) = Tmp_Amplitude(0,:,:,:) + TmpNew(:,:,:) * Htime

        VMx_Av = VMx_Av + VMxNew * Htime
        VMy_Av = VMy_Av + VMyNew * Htime
        VMz_Av = VMz_Av + VMzNew * Htime
        Tmp_Av = Tmp_Av + TmpNew * Htime
        Prs_Av = Prs_Av + Prs    * Htime

        Do i=1,N_Fourier
            Tmp_Amplitude( i,:,:,:) = Tmp_Amplitude( i,:,:,:) + TmpNew(:,:,:) * Htime * cos( Omega(i) * TimCur )
            Tmp_Amplitude(-i,:,:,:) = Tmp_Amplitude(-i,:,:,:) + TmpNew(:,:,:) * Htime * sin( Omega(i) * TimCur )
        End Do
    End Subroutine Fourier_sum

    Subroutine Plot_Amplitude
        Character(len=10), Dimension(-3:3) :: Title

        Write (*,*) ' Plot_Amplitude'

        Open(123, file='Amplitudes.dat', status='unknown', form='formatted')
        Open(124, file='Average_velocity.ddd', status='unknown', form='unformatted')

        ! Arrays are now (Y,Z,X) = (j,k,i); Tmp_Amplitude is (L, Y, Z, X).
        Tmp_Amplitude(0,0:Ny2,0:Nz2,0:Nx2) = Tmp_Amplitude(0,0:Ny2,0:Nz2,0:Nx2) / Time_Interval + Teta(0:Ny2,0:Nz2,0:Nx2)

        VMx_Av = VMx_Av / Time_Interval
        VMy_Av = VMy_Av / Time_Interval
        VMz_Av = VMz_Av / Time_Interval
        Tmp_Av = Tmp_Av / Time_Interval
        Prs_Av = Prs_Av / Time_Interval

        Do i=1,N_Fourier
            Tmp_Amplitude( i,:,:,:) = 2.d0 * Tmp_Amplitude( i,:,:,:) / Time_Interval
            Tmp_Amplitude(-i,:,:,:) = 2.d0 * Tmp_Amplitude(-i,:,:,:) / Time_Interval
        End Do

        Title(0) = 'Average   '
        Title(1) = 'Omega1_cos';  Title(-1) = 'Omega1_sin'
        Title(2) = 'Omega2_cos';  Title(-2) = 'Omega2_sin'
        Title(3) = 'Omega3_cos';  Title(-3) = 'Omega3_sin'

        Mx=(Nx2+1)/2
        Write (123, *) 'VARIABLES = "X","Y","Z"', (',"',Title(i),'"', i=-N_fourier, N_fourier)
        !              Write (123,301) Mx, Mx, Mx
        Write (123,301) Nx2+1, Ny2+1, Nz2+1

        ! ********* Write the result *******************

        Do k=0,Nz2
            Do j=0,Ny2
                Do i=0,Nx2
                    ! Same point order in the file as before; only the in-memory
                    ! index order changed: (L, x, y, z) -> (L, y, z, x).
                    Write (123,310) X12(i), Y12(j), Z12(k), (Tmp_Amplitude(L,j,k,i), L=-N_fourier, N_fourier)
                End Do
            End Do
        End Do
        Rewind 124
        ! Keep the LEGACY file byte order (x fastest, then y, then z) so files
        ! remain readable by the original tools. Only the in-memory index order
        ! of the arrays changed to (j,k,i).
        Write (124) ((( VMx_Av(j,k,i), i=0,Nx1), j=0,Ny2), k=0,Nz2)
        Write (124) ((( VMy_Av(j,k,i), i=0,Nx2), j=0,Ny1), k=0,Nz2)
        Write (124) ((( VMz_Av(j,k,i), i=0,Nx2), j=0,Ny2), k=0,Nz1)
        Write (124) ((( Tmp_Av(j,k,i), i=0,Nx2), j=0,Ny2), k=0,Nz2)
        Write (124) ((( Prs_Av(j,k,i), i=1,Nx1), j=1,Ny1), k=1,Nz1)

        VMx = VMx_Av;  VMy = VMy_Av;  VMz = VMz_Av;  Tmpr = Tmp_Av; Prs = Prs_Av

        Tmpr = Tmpr + Teta
        Call Point_Write ( Nx2, Ny2, Nz2, Tmpr,   X12, Y12, Z12, 51, 'Tmpr      ')
        Call Point_Write ( Nx1, Ny2, Nz2, VMxNew, X  , Y12, Z12, 71, 'Vx        ')
        Call Point_Write ( Nx2, Ny1, Nz2, VMyNew, X12, Y  , Z12, 81, 'Vy        ')
        Call Point_Write ( Nx2, Ny2, Nz1, VMzNew, X12, Y12, Z  , 91, 'Vz        ')
        !      Call Point_Write ( Nx, Ny, Nz, Prs,    X12(1:Nx1), Y12(1:Ny1), Z12(1:Nz1), 61, 'Prs       ')

        Write (*,*) ' Plot_Amplitude'
        Return
        301        Format ('ZONE F=POINT, I=',I4, ', J=',I4, ', K=',I4)
        310        Format ( 12(E12.5,1x) )

    End Subroutine Plot_Amplitude

=== GOLDEN_TEST_FULL_SOURCE Sun Nov  8 07:08:46 PM EST 2026 ===
     1	// Fresh Fortran/C++ runs with matching inputs and format-aware comparisons.
     2	#include <gtest/gtest.h>
     3	#include "GoldenSupport.h"
     4	#include <chrono>
     5	#include <iostream>
     6	
     7	#ifndef CFD_CPP_EXE
     8	#define CFD_CPP_EXE "CFD_CPP_EXE_NOT_SET"
     9	#endif
    10	#ifndef CFD_FORTRAN_EXE
    11	#define CFD_FORTRAN_EXE "CFD_FORTRAN_EXE_NOT_SET"
    12	#endif
    13	#ifndef CFD_FORTRAN_BASE_CONV
    14	#define CFD_FORTRAN_BASE_CONV "CFD_FORTRAN_BASE_CONV_NOT_SET"
    15	#endif
    16	#ifndef CFD_GOLDEN_CONFIG_DIR
    17	#define CFD_GOLDEN_CONFIG_DIR "CFD_GOLDEN_CONFIG_DIR_NOT_SET"
    18	#endif
    19	
    20	namespace {
    21	using namespace cfd_golden;
    22	struct GoldenScenario { std::string name, cppConfigFileName; bool timeAveraging = false; bool writeFieldSnapshots = false; };
    23	
    24	std::vector<GoldenScenario> discoverGoldenScenarios() {
    25	    std::vector<GoldenScenario> scenarios;
    26	    const fs::path dir(CFD_GOLDEN_CONFIG_DIR);
    27	    const std::string suffix = ".cpp.json";
    28	    if (!fs::is_directory(dir)) return scenarios;
    29	    for (const auto& entry : fs::directory_iterator(dir)) {
    30	        const auto name = entry.path().filename().string();
    31	        if (entry.is_regular_file() && name.size() > suffix.size() &&
    32	            name.compare(name.size() - suffix.size(), suffix.size(), suffix) == 0) {
    33	            // Read each scenario's own time_averaging.enabled and
    34	            // output.write_field_snapshots so the test body below knows,
    35	            // per scenario, whether to also check the _av family and the
    36	            // snapshot family -- both are written only when their flag is
    37	            // on, so comparing them unconditionally would fail on the
    38	            // scenarios that legitimately never produce them.
    39	            const Json cfg = readJson(entry.path());
    40	            const bool ta = cfg.value("time_averaging", Json::object()).value("enabled", false);
    41	            const bool snap = cfg.value("output", Json::object()).value("write_field_snapshots", false);
    42	            scenarios.push_back({name.substr(0, name.size() - suffix.size()), name, ta, snap});
    43	        }
    44	    }
    45	    std::sort(scenarios.begin(), scenarios.end(),
    46	        [](const GoldenScenario& a, const GoldenScenario& b) { return a.name < b.name; });
    47	    return scenarios;
    48	}
    49	
    50	int runCommand(const std::string& command, const fs::path& cwd) {
    51	    return std::system(("cd " + shellQuote(cwd) + " && " + command).c_str());
    52	}
    53	
    54	class GoldenValueTest : public ::testing::TestWithParam<GoldenScenario> {};
    55	
    56	TEST_P(GoldenValueTest, MatchesFortranReference) {
    57	    const auto& scenario = GetParam();
    58	    ASSERT_TRUE(fs::is_regular_file(CFD_CPP_EXE)) << "Set CFD_CPP_EXE to the C++ executable";
    59	    ASSERT_TRUE(fs::is_regular_file(CFD_FORTRAN_EXE)) << "Set CFD_FORTRAN_EXE to the Fortran executable";
    60	    ASSERT_TRUE(fs::is_regular_file(CFD_FORTRAN_BASE_CONV)) << "Set CFD_FORTRAN_BASE_CONV to Conv.dat";
    61	    const auto root = uniqueRunDirectory(scenario.name);
    62	    const auto cppDir = root / "cpp", fortranDir = root / "fortran";
    63	    fs::create_directory(cppDir);
    64	    fs::create_directory(fortranDir);
    65	    SCOPED_TRACE("Scenario " + scenario.name + "; retained inputs, outputs and logs: " + root.string());
    66	    std::cout << "Golden comparison files: " << root << std::endl;
    67	
    68	    Json config = readJson(fs::path(CFD_GOLDEN_CONFIG_DIR) / scenario.cppConfigFileName);
    69	    config["output"]["directory"] = cppDir.string();
    70	    const auto cppConfig = cppDir / "config.json";
    71	    writeText(cppConfig, config.dump(2) + "\n");
    72	    // Render and validate ALL inputs before either expensive solver starts.
    73	    writeText(fortranDir / "Conv.dat", renderConvDat(config, CFD_FORTRAN_BASE_CONV));
    74	    const auto fortranStart = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    75	    ASSERT_EQ(0, runCommand(shellQuote(fs::absolute(CFD_FORTRAN_EXE)) + " > " +
    76	        shellQuote(fortranDir / "run.log") + " 2>&1", fortranDir)) << "Fortran failed; inspect run.log";
    77	    const double fortranSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - fortranStart).count();
    78	
    79	    const auto echo = verifyFortranEcho(fortranDir / "Conv.out", config);
    80	    ASSERT_EQ(0u, echo.mismatches) << echo.summary();
    81	
    82	    const auto cppStart = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    83	    ASSERT_EQ(0, runCommand(shellQuote(fs::absolute(CFD_CPP_EXE)) + " " + shellQuote(cppConfig) + " > " +
    84	        shellQuote(cppDir / "run.log") + " 2>&1", cppDir)) << "C++ failed; inspect run.log";
    85	    const double cppSeconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - cppStart).count();
    86	
    87	    // The GPU port's entire reason to exist is speed, not just correctness
    88	    // (data stays resident on the GPU across iterations, every physics/
    89	    // solver step runs as a kernel, instead of Fortran's per-step CPU work)
    90	    // -- so a slower cfdSolver is a regression worth failing the build over,
    91	    // same as a wrong number would be. EXPECT, not ASSERT: a timing miss
    92	    // shouldn't hide whether the output was also still correct.
    93	    std::cout << "Timing (" << scenario.name << "): Fortran=" << fortranSeconds << "s  cfdSolver="
    94	        << cppSeconds << "s  speedup=" << (fortranSeconds / cppSeconds) << "x" << std::endl;
    95	    EXPECT_LE(cppSeconds, fortranSeconds) << "cfdSolver (" << cppSeconds << "s) was slower than Fortran ("
    96	        << fortranSeconds << "s) for scenario " << scenario.name;
    97	
    98	    std::vector<std::string> files = {"Conv.out", "History.dat", "Serie.dat", "Psi_Yaverage.dat", "Tmpr_Yaverage.dat"};
    99	    if (scenario.timeAveraging) {
   100	        // finalizeTimeAverage()'s in-place substitution already means the
   101	        // five files above reflect the time-averaged state when this flag
   102	        // is on (that path gets exercised either way) -- these four add
   103	        // direct coverage of the _av family's own writers. Deliberately
   104	        // NOT included: Amplitudes.dat (a different column count per
   105	        // configured frequency; compareTables assumes exactly 3 columns)
   106	        // and Average_velocity.ddd (gfortran unformatted-binary, not
   107	        // Tecplot text at all) -- both need their own comparator, not
   108	        // written yet.
   109	        for (const char* f : {"Tmpr_av.dat", "Vx_av.dat", "Vy_av.dat", "Vz_av.dat"}) files.push_back(f);
   110	    }
   111	    if (scenario.writeFieldSnapshots) {
   112	        // Alex's own disabled Point_Write block (now re-enabled in
   113	        // Solution_time_Q2D.f90) and cfdSolver's writeFieldSnapshots() both
   114	        // write these six fields as one final, ghost-inclusive snapshot at
   115	        // the end of the run, in the same 4-column X,Y,Z,value Tecplot
   116	        // format as the _av family above -- so compareTables3D handles
   117	        // them too (see the dispatch below).
   118	        for (const char* f : {"Tmpr.dat", "Vx.dat", "Vy.dat", "Vz.dat", "Pres.dat", "Potential.dat"}) files.push_back(f);
   119	    }
   120	    for (const std::string& file : files) {
   121	        try {
   122	            Comparison result;
   123	            const bool avFamily = file.size() > 7 && file.compare(file.size() - 7, 7, "_av.dat") == 0;
   124	            const bool snapshotFamily = file == "Tmpr.dat" || file == "Vx.dat" || file == "Vy.dat" ||
   125	                file == "Vz.dat" || file == "Pres.dat" || file == "Potential.dat";
   126	            if (file == "Conv.out") {
   127	                // See compareReports()'s own skipLabels doc comment: when
   128	                // averaging is on, Fortran double-counts Teta's
   129	                // contribution to exactly these two numbers (its own
   130	                // substitution runs before they're computed, and they
   131	                // already have their own analytic Teta term); cfdSolver's
   132	                // number is the internally-consistent one here, not
   133	                // Fortran's.
   134	                std::vector<std::string> skip;
   135	                if (scenario.timeAveraging) skip = {"Nusselt number", "Midplane Nusselt number"};
   136	                result = compareReports(fortranDir / file, cppDir / file, skip);
   137	            }
   138	            else if (avFamily || snapshotFamily) result = compareTables3D(fortranDir / file, cppDir / file);
   139	            else result = compareTables(fortranDir / file, cppDir / file, file.find("Yaverage") != std::string::npos);
   140	            EXPECT_EQ(0u, result.mismatches) << file << ": " << result.summary();
   141	        } catch (const std::exception& e) {
   142	            ADD_FAILURE() << file << ": " << e.what();
   143	        }
   144	    }
   145	}
   146	
   147	INSTANTIATE_TEST_SUITE_P(
   148	    NonRedundantConfigurations, GoldenValueTest, ::testing::ValuesIn(discoverGoldenScenarios()),
   149	    [](const ::testing::TestParamInfo<GoldenScenario>& info) { return info.param.name; });
   150	} // namespace
